Posts for 2014-01

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Wed Jan 01 2014 11:35:47 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

AK had told all volunteers to speak lie that every family will get 21000 liters per month of water free, and AK had told all volunteers to hide the FACT that 1 liter extra would cost them Rs 700 per month !! AK had also told them to hide the fact that slum dwellers with no meter will get NO benefits from this scheme

The 100 AAP page manifesto did say in fine print in foot notes that "21000 liters free, but 21001 liter extra would cost Rs 700 per month" !!! But AK had told all volunteers to hide this fact from public. AK had also told volunteers to create a mirage and fake hopes in slum dwellers that APP deliver 21000 liters per family per month free via tankers !! AK himself never told in any public meeting that 1 liter extra would cost Rs 700 per month !!!

When Janlokpal sells country to MNC-owners, and MNC-owners loot away every penny, AK will say same thing. "See I promised you no corruption !! Now you are all slaves with no money to give bribe, and hence there is no corruption anymore" !!!

All in all , "it was written in manifesto" but never told in public meeting is the dhokha aka cheating that companies do by writing terms and conditions in fine print that no one can read. Its like saying "life insurance of Rs 1 crore for just rs 10000" and then writing in fine print "only if you die on sunday after you are 80 years old !!"

#AAPisCongress3 .

====

This is how AK's free water, free power economic policies will reduce corruption --- his economic polices will make all pauper, penniless and bankrupt. So no one will have any money to pay bribes. And thus, there will be no corruption !!!

Read full post →

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Wed Jan 01 2014 12:16:03 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Proposal to deliver CHEAP not free water to slum dwellers --- give a metered connection to slum, and make a local recallable person in-charge of that connection. Allow him to sell water for say 50 paise per bucket.

Here is a quick-fix fast way to deliver water to slums. DJB = Delhi Jal Board can appoint one person in every locality. That person will be replaceable by locals via SMS and/or visit to Patwari office. (see section-6.6. of rahulmehta . com/301.htm for technical details of Right to Recall procedures) . The person owning that connection should be legally allowed to sell water at say 50 paise per bucket of 20 liters i.e. Rs 25 per kiloliter. DJB can charge him money for water, say Rs 10 per kiloliter and rest he can keep as his costs and profits. Since that person is recallable and replaceable by voters, he will give proper service to customers. As per price, nothing is free. The person can get change from RBI by busloads, and give Rs 50 to Rs 100 worth change to each local, and later same 25 paise coins will be used.

That person may even create a small tank and multiple taps where can store and give water for a longer duration across day.

Read full post →

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Thu Jan 02 2014 03:13:08 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Nationalists such as BST-workers , RSS-workers refused to supportthe proposal to print Right to Recall Electricity Regulator , Electricity Minister . As a result, corruption in electricity dept grew , electricity pricess rose, and now foreign agents like AK will gain big time when electricity prices drop in april-2014

Pls see chap-42 of rahulmehta . com / 301.htm . This suggestion has been in the previous edition of the book, which came out in dec-2008. It is fact that there is rampant corruption in electricity Ministry, and as a result, the production costs are high and supply costs are much much higher. And it is also a fact that BJP\Congress leaders made tons of money , and so did distribution company owners like Tata, Ambani, Torrent etc. The electricity rates without taxes be Rs 2.50 per unit, and with taxes, shiuld not exceed Rs 3.50 per unit. But Delhi rates , Gujarat rates and rates across India have been much much higher.

The reason for rates was lack of Right to Recall over Electricity Regulators and Electricity Ministers (and of course Lack of RTR over CM). And so CM, Electricity Minister and Electricity Regulators never ordered proper audit on generation and distribution company. And why do we have NO RTR over these positions? The biggest reason I see is --- BST-workers , RSS-workers , VHP-workers, Bajrang-Dal-workers despite nationalism, refused to support the proposal to print RTR-Electricity-Regulator and RTR-Electricity-Regulator proposals in gazette.

Now the foreigners took advantage of this fact. They installed their agent AK as CM. He has ordered audit on electricity company. The audit will reveal that Distribution companies were over charging by at least 35% to 50% and were siphoning all profits into sister companies. And with that, AK may manage to get court order to reduce the electricity prices. And AK will become hero nationwide.

OR, the MNC-owners can blackmail NaMo to give 300 NDA\BJP tickets to MNC-agents and Missionary agents in BJP\NDA. If NaMo agrees, then MNC-owners will create weak audit report which will not show much over-charging. In this case, AK will fall flat and NaMo's gains will be protected.

But in either case, Nationalist , Swadeshi and Hinduvaadies have lost.

The solution I propose is that BST-workers, RSS-workers, VHP-workers, Banjrang-workers etc should take zero tolerance against corruption, lack of transparency and should support and campaign for EACH and EVERY law-draft that can benefit India. If they fail to do so, the corruption in administration\courts will NOT reduce. And then the foreigners will always manage to take advantage of corruption, damage it causes, feelings against corruption and use that feeling to install their agents.

I would give analogy. During 800 AD to 1100 AD, Indian money landers used to charge heavy interest and debt was generational i.e. if father fails to repay debt then the son will have to pay the debt !! Like that, many families were in debt for generations. The Muslim Maulavies and Kings offered them freedom from debt under condition that they accept Islam. And many accepted Islam to get rid of slavery caused by interest !! I cant get the exact quote, but even Vivekanda criticized uncontrolled usury in India of 800 AD till his times. If Hindu nationalists back then had worked to control usury, then many lesser would have converted.

Same way, is fact Indian elitemen are robbing day and night from Mineral Royalties, by bribing PM, CMs, MPs, MLAs, IAS, IPS etc , by bribing judges etc etc. As a result, land prices are high, judges are slow and corrupt, judges promote usury and exploitation, and so this creates opportunity for foreign agents like AK to expand. Solution I propose is that BST-workers, RSS-workers, Bajrang-Dal-workers, VHP-workers should promote MRCM-draft to reduce theft in Mineral Royalties, promote RTR/Jury-drafts to reduce theft in administration, courts and police , and promote wealth tax and inheritance to reduce land prices. Otherwise either MNC-owners will manage to cut NaMo votes or MNC-owners will be able to force NaMo to give tickets to MNC-agents. In either cases, Nationalists would lose.

Read full post →

So in feb-2014, MNC-owners will give two deals to NaMo

1. Give tickets to 400 MNC-agents and Missionary-agents in BJP\NDA

OR

2. CAG will disclose the data, and enable AK to get court order to reduce electricity charges by 30% and paid-media will make it a news bigger than freedom of India, and enable AK to cut crores of votes across India.

And they should ask crores of voters to do the same. RTR over these positions will reduce over-charging and bring down electricity prices.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Thu Jan 02 2014 09:27:29 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

How AAP returned the slum votes back to Congress --- Because free water to middle class is insult to injury to slum dwellers and most slum dwellers get electricity from private sub-meters and so they wont get any discounts , and their bill will increase !!! Solution? See last para

Delhi has 1.2 crore voters and total population of 2.2 crore. But Delhi has only 32 lakh house hold meters, i.e. average of about 7 family members per meters. Now many homes have 0 members (closed flats, and there are lakhs of them), 1-2 family members or just 3-4 family members. So how come there are 7 family members per meter? Thats because in many slum areas, the slumlord has just one electricity meter, and he gives private sub-meters to 100s of slum dwellers or charges flat rate. Now slum lord's bill is above 400 units per month, and so his charges will now increase. He will pass the raise on slum dwellers. And hence electricity bill of many slum dwellers will now increase !!! So in Loksabha elections, they will vote for Congress !!

Plus, the slum dwellers who get water for just 20-30 minutes a day or get erratic tankers will have royal heartburn when they hear that people who live in well to do small houses will get 20000 liters for free, as many with 2-3 members consume below 20000 liters a month !! Further, the commercial water users' rates have increased. So many slums which get water from private commercial tankers will now have to pay MORE for water !!! So these slum voters will be back to Congress, and will vote for Congress with more vengeance.

But a big chunk of former BJP voters who live in small flats will come to AAP, because their electricity bill slightly reduced and they are getting water for free !!!

So across India, paid-media will project AAP as anti-corruption hero. So middle class votes across India will go for AAP. While slum votes will remain with Congress and now Congress will actively tell slum dwellers that "see, AK gave free water to middle class and NOTHING to slum dwellers".

SOLUTION : IMO, all committed nationalists such as BST-workers, RSS-workers, VHP-workers etc should promote law-drafts that will reduce land prices, construction prices and thus enable slum dwellers can buy flats. How? Pls see MRCM draft in chap-5 and wealth tax law-draft in chap-25 of rahulmehta . com / 301.htm . And they should publciize REAL anti-corruption law-drafts like Jury System, Right to recall etc etc.

As long as there are poor, the MNC-owners via paid-media will manage to install fake pro-poor leaders like Congressi and AAP and get votes. As long as there is corruption, MNC-owners via paid-media will manage to install fake heroes like AK. Solution is to promote REAL anti-poverty and anti-corruption law-drafts like RTR, JurySys, MRCM, wealth tax, inheritance tax etc etc

Read full post →

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Fri Jan 03 2014 13:16:20 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

(1) AK opposes the proposal to let slum dwellers select tanker providers via SMS or person visit to Patwari office !! Reason AK cited was "we need to focus ONLY on middle class votes" !! (2) Meanwhile, Congress workers in ahmedabad, tell slum dwellers that AAP is middle-class party by citing "free water to middle class and nothing for slums" law of Delhi !!

Following is the proposal one RTR-activists in Delhi took to AK

1. Allow a Delhi voter, whose house doesnt have connection, to register his cell number at local Patwari office or Civic center. It can be one per family.

2. Issue numbers of each tanker provider (they already have it)

3. The slum dweller can specify/change the tanker provider any day via SMS or personally visiting Patwari office

4. Delhi Jal Board will give say 200 liter per day per family who has allocated the water quota for free

5. The slum dwellers can pay the carrier charges to tanker owner , say 25 paise per bucket or whatever.

The fact that citizens can change tanker owner via SMS will ensure that tanker owners will provide optimal service.

AK tore apart the paper on which the proposal was written into some 20 pieces !! Not only he called it "fairy tale", but he asked all his volunteers to dismiss this proposal as "fairy tale" !! AK made useless excuses like "garib aadami mobile kahan se layega" (where will poor get mobile from?) , despite the fact proposed procedure allows person to change tanker provider via personal visit to Patwari office. And he made all excuses like "what if person's mobile gets stolen and some tanker mafia sends SMS ete etc !!" Some 2000 slum dwellers sent SMS #AllowSlumDwellerToChooseTankerProvider at AAP's voting number 0-88061-10335 ; AK refused to disclose the SMS count for that tag !!! When an AAP MLA supported this proposal, AK replied "now we have to focus on nationwide middle class voters ONLY ; hell with slum voters !!! "

Anyway, AK is not interested in this proposal. Now if YOU are interested in this proposal, then I request you all to ask Delhi voters to send #AllowSlumDwellerToChooseTankerProvider at AAP's voting number 0-88061-10335 ;

=====

The Congress workers in slum areas have started RIGHT propaganda that "AK has given free 20000 liters of water per month to families living in flats, but zero free water to slum dwellers". In fact, water prices for slum dwellers have increased by 10% because private tankers now charge 10% more , as commercial water meters cost 10% more starting 1-jan-2014. So all in all, AAP wont get any slum votes in Gujarat or rest of India for that matter, and will be mainly cutting middle class votes only.

Moral of the story is -- NEVER NEVER NEVER EVER spend time in criticizing small steps of leader who has support of paid media. Throw ONLY major sensible demands, which are USEFUL for nation at large ; so that if under pressure the leader does execute the demand , then at least nation benefits ; and if leader doesnt execute the demand then the leader gets exposed.

I would give analogy on why one should never chase small mistakes of leaders. When lizard is chased by predator, the lizard will leave off its tail to engage the predator so that lizard can escape. Like that, leaders make small mistakes so create a controversy so that focus moves away from larger compromises, scams. nefarious deals etc.

Now the next drama is on shiela dixit's inquiries. Please note that AK will soon order inquiry of Shiela Dixit. And AK may even order inquiry on Robert Vadra . After all , its ONLY a six months drama. So PLEASE DO NOT MAKE MISTAKE OF ASKING FOR PLAIN VANILLA INQUIRY ON SHIELA DIXIT. Instead, ask for Trial by Jury of 1500 Delhi voters between the age of 40 years and 55 years chosen at random from voter list, in which Jury may demand narco-test in public if Jury feels necessary. AK will oppose this demand and thus AK will get exposed, that he is just another Congress-like or BJP-like rundown politician posing as Bhagat Singh. But if all you demand is plain vanilla inquiry by some judges, most of whom are corrupt and nepotic and compromised in many ways. The inquiry like 2G scam will go on for 5 years and then case will go on for 5 years and then appeals will go on 25 years and by that time Sheila Dixit may not be even alive. So AK has nothing to lose by ordering such useless inquiry.

The ex-Ministers are corrupt, but not stupid. They are cunning and shrewd anough not to leave any paper trail. All you will end up getting is "oversight or improper use of discretionary powers" which doesnt amount to any major crime. And without narco-tests in public, there is no way to know where they have hidden their assets.

Now is inquiry and trial by Jury of 1500 voters possible? Well, if Greece of 800 BC could have inquiry and trial by 1500 Jurors chosen at random from population, it is surely possible today. The paid-intellectuals give all sorts of hoarse cries against Jury System, because the elitemen who pay them want judge system and not Jury System. Also the paid-intellectuals oppose narco-test in public in high corruption cases. Now narco-test is not 100% reliable, but then I kever said that narco should be taken as evidence. But narco can lead to material assets and evidence. eg during narco test, a corrupt minister may end up revealing where he has buried his gold or some diary, and that gold/diary can become material asset and evidence. And statements made in narco need not be used as final proofs. IOW, naroc has ONLY plus points and no minus points. But paid-intellectuals oppose narco-test in public in corruption cases as they get paid by people like Sonia Ghandy, Sharad Pawar etc etc

Now AK had opposed narco-test in public of A Raja, Shahid Balwa etc etc. In apr-2011, he had opposed narco-test in public by citing human rights nonsense and supreme court judgment. The supreme court judgment is now NOT a show stopper, as Assembly Speaker has powers to order arrest and powers to conduct narco-test. YES, Assembly Speaker has all powers that judges have, and judges cannot give stay order on Assembly Speaker's orders. So via resolution in Assembly, Assembly Speaker can order arrest and narco-test in public on anyone and everyone.

Now if you ask AK to conduct trial on corrupt ex-Ministers , via Jury Trial using narco, AK will oppose it again. And that will p;rove to public that he is just another Congress/BJP like career leader. If you ask for inquiry on ex-Ministers via corrupt/nepotic judges and using no-narco-tests, then next day he will order this inquiry and will win the round.

Now what if AK indeed orders Jury Trial on ex-Ministers with narco-test in public? Well, all glory for AK - nation got benefited and I will donate Rs 100,000 to AAP. Not only it will benefit one case, but start a new tradition.

===

So all in all, pls dont demand plain vanilla inquiry on ex-Ministers like Shiela Dixit. Pls order inquiry by Jury of 1500 votrs chosen at random from Delhi's voter list between the age of 40 years and 55 years, and using narco-test in public. If you make mistake of demanding plain vanilla inquiry by some corrupt nepotic judge, then AK will win that battle just as AK won 10 BHK controversy.

Here I would point out one difference between many honest activists and dishonest crooks like AK. And I made it clear way back in oct-1998 itself that winning elections useless as people change after winning --- STRONG good laws like RTR, Jury etc can come only via real mass-movements. So when I contested elections in may-2009, there was no backstabbing anyone. And for 10 years, many RSS-activists etc who likes RTR etc did NOT support me, because they feared that my contesting elections can cut middle class votes and damage BJP. And Congress-supporters did NOT support me because they feared that my pro-poor proposals like MRCM, wealth tax will cut congress votes !! I was aware about the FACT that if I announce election-contesting, then RSS-activists , BJP-supporters, Congress-supporters will not support me. And if I lie to them and tell them in oct-1998 that I will not contest elections, then I can get ample support from both.

Why did AK make fake promise that he would never contest elections? This was mainly because he wanted support from middle class NaMo-activists, RSS-activists, BJP-activists etc. None would have supported AK if they knew that AK is going to contest elections. All in all, they all got backstabbed.

One may ask --- whats wrong in back-stabbing BJP, given that they are all corrupt anyway? Well, it is not BJP who got back-stabbed, it is BJP-activists who got backstabbed. And most activists across all parties are honest -- they campaign for their respective party as they see their party as least bad along some important dimension. And if a man can backstab someone, tomorrow he can backstab anyone and everyone. Because backstabbing is a personality trait, it is not a strategic choice.

Anyway, tentative election results date are 18-may-2014. Lets who more get backstabbed in jun-2014

Then why arent AAP MLAs selling out en-masse? If 15 out of 28 AAP MLAs join BJP or Congress, then none will lose MLA seat and can get hefty amount. Thats because buyers are having cold feet. Whichever party that makes offer will lose Loksabha votes big time. So each party is waiting for Loksabhai elections to end and then make an offer.

Once Loksabha elections end on around 18-may-2014, the auctioning of AAP MLAs will start. Things will depend on outcome of elections.

AK has opposed proposal to print RTR-MLA-drafts, RTR-MP-draft, RTR-PM-draft, RTR-CM-draft, wealth tax drafts, inheritance tax drafts, drafts to expel Bangladeshies etc AAP loksabha manifesto.

Solution I propose is that (1) if you still believe in AAP, pls send SMS to AAP's number to print these drafts in Gazette . Pls put #hashcode in SMS. You will not even get count of #hashcodes back !! This will prove that they are not interested at all (2) If you have no faith AAP, then pls ask as many voters as possible to send orders to MPs via SMS to print these drafts in the Gazette.

Proposals like "draftless bring black money back in 1 year" only increases AAP support --- because "draftless BBM in 1 year" is flat impossibility , and many professional like CA , lawyers etc will simply join AAP if such proposals are mooted by BJP

If today, my Right to Recall Group says "we will make Indian Military as strong as USA Military in one year" and dont give any drafts, then such proposals will not increase our membership, but make our members walk to AAP offices !! Why? Because to anyone who is aware of engineering, such proposals will look like "lets become pilot and get the golden pie in the sky" !! An engineer will take such proposal as attempt to misguide voters. And that will only push him away from me. At the same time, when I told activists that "MRCM will zero poverty in 4 months" , they disbelieved, but AFTER they saw the MRCM draft, they agreed. And when I said that "wealth tax will reduce 2BHK to 4BHK apartments prices in metro cities to 1/4th of what it is now in 6 months" , the activists laughed. But after seeing the wealth tax DRAFT and all numbers, they whole heartedly agreed. So if one is making tall claims, he better have drafts. Draftless tall claims will only look like attempt to misguide voters.

Now let me give you some details on how black money is hoarded in Mauritius bank or Swiss bank. Say I am a corrupt Minister. Then I will not put money in my name. I will have a relative like trusted son or nephew who is citizen of say Luxumberg and not India. That relative will open account and I will only have power of attorney. So technically money is not in my name. So when Govt of India asks Swiss banks "how many Indian citizens have money in Swiss bank", my relative's name will not even come in list , as he is citizen of Lumomberg !! And Siwss bank will not even reply for years to begin with.

Another way is that people form Trusts and companies, and oner partner is non-Indian-citizen. And money is kept in the name of trusts and companies and that non-Indian-citizen is used as dummy. Lastly, Swiss banks and Mauritius banks have vaults, in which money is stashed in gold, platinum, diamonds, USA's bearer bonds etc and bank officially has no knowledge of how much money is stashed in bank.

So getting information alone will take years. Getting money is another big game.

Now people like CA, economists , lawyers, activists etc are well aware of these details. So unless someone shows me and them a DRAFT of how black dollars will come back in 1 year or even 2-3 years, a promise like " I will bring black dollars back in 1 year" will only look like attempt to misguide voters. And this will make such well informed and well aware person join AAP.

Now many dont know that AK is foreign agent. They do NOT know that AK's media coverage is paid by American elitemen, Missionaries etc etc. To them, AK is honest IITian, an honest ex Income Tax commissioner etc out to reform the system. And in such a mirage, if they see BJP claiming without DRAFT "we will bring black money back in 1 year", they will see BJP as party making fake promises to misguide voters and they will see AAP as making down to earth promises. And since they dont that AK is foreign agent, they will see AAP as less bad option.

So pls provide DRAFTS of the tall promises you or make. OR pls focus on EASY solutions. eg wealth tax of 1% on land prices above 250 sqft per person, and "sale of land by auction only" can reduce black rupees in India to 10% of what is now in just 6 months. The drafts are all ready to the last level of comma and full stop.

And if one still wants to make DRAFTLESS tall promises, then well, more and more aware youth and more and more professional will join AAP and no one can stop that.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Mon Jan 06 2014 01:18:37 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

AK had asked AAP volunteers to tell to slum dwellers that each slum dweller family will get coupon book of 21000 liters of water every month, similar to some ration card or milk coupons, and tankers will deliver them free 700 liters everyday for each coupon !!!

I always wondered how can one fool a family with no meter that they will get 21000 liters a month of free water. Wont at least 1 out of 100 person ask question? So I asked AAP volunteers how they managed to fool slum dwellers to believe that unmetered connections will also get 21000 liters a month per family for free !! After all, to give any limited quantity for free, one needs a meter. The volunteers tell me that AK had fooled volunteers into believing that each slum dweller family will get some kind of coupon booklet every month, and each coupon booklet will have some 200 coupons, each coupon having value of 100 liters = 5 buckets of water !!! The volunteers were even shown samples of coupon booklets, and they showed such samples to slum dwellers when slum dwellers would ask them this water distribution question !! One volunteer has got election pamphlet with AK's pix, jhadu, candidate name etc etc and promise of this water-coupon booklet and also picture of this water coupon booklet !!! He will dig that pamphlet out and send me scanned copy.

Over 80% volunteers were all unpaid, genuinely prop-poor and concerned citizens. Many took a month's unpaid leave, lost salary of as high as Rs 40000 a month, spent Rs 500 of their own a day in hotel, spent Rs 300 a day for their own food, paid for their travel and also donated money to AAP.

And this is what they ended up doing ---- fooling slum dwellers into believing that slum dwellers will get water coupon booklet of 21000 liters per month per family !!!

===

In share market, we have such 10s of scams where a start up will come, promise "1 ka 10" in one year and fool 1000s of investors. In such cases I studied, I saw that main person who ran the scam was NOT the company owner, but some guy at apex position with lot of media under him. That apex person would not anywhere on any official document of the company. That guy via paid-media will create a hype and faith in investors in that company. And then brokers will sell the shares en-masse. In each case, the company owner is shown as some whizkid and miracle guy. Here activists = grassroot brokers were all honest --- they themselves were fooled by paid-media. But middle level of brokers, who sell news in paid media got hefty payments.

I remember reading about one company in US which had "developed" "technology" for farming grains, vegetables and pulses at the bottom of the ocean !!! Their theme was "once world on surface runs out of land to do agriculture, the land at the bottom of ocean will be used for farming, and at that time, this company will make huge profits". It sold shares worth millions of dollars, and would be a billion plus by today's level. The owner of that company was a high profile scientist.

AK = AAP story is more or less same. The American elitemen, some Indian elitemen like Jindal, and Missionaries via paid-media is selling dreams to genuine activists and squeezing their time , energy and also small amounts of donations. The money they get from concerned citizens is small . In fact, for every Rs 1000 crore American elitemen and Jindals spend on paid-media, the donation they get is just Rs 100 crore. So goal is NOT monetary profits. But goal is to ensure that activists do NOT work on genuine economic , political reforms that can damage American elitemen's and Missionaries' interests in India.

AK appoints Digvijay Singh's trusted ex-IAS officer to draft Mohalla Sabha draft !!!

paid-Economic-Times says so. See the full news with link below.

I wont waste your time in telling you about Congress leader Digvijay Singh. Now what type of IAS will become key trusted man of Digvijay Singh? Well, that IAS will now draft the MOST IMPORTANT bill of AAP.

In Mohalla sabha draft, delhi of 1.2 crore votes will be divided into 2500 Mohalla, each having about 4000 to 5000 voters. They will meet once a month, They cant submit their complaint via net or SMS, but must come in person. There will be voting on each small proposal !! The head of Mohalla Sabha will be elected and recallable. Recall would require signature of 3000 voters, and there will be NO SMS interface for recall. The State Election will verify signatures even though State Election Commission has NO signature specimen of even 1% of the record. Budget of Mohalla Sabha will be about Rs 4 lakhs per year !! So 4000 voters will spend 2 hrs a month i.e. 24 hrs a month to decide how Rs 4 lakhs will be spend. That is Rs 400000 / (4000 * 24 * 2) = Rs 100/48 = about Rs 2 per hour. IOW, one human hour will be spent to decide how Rs 2 will be spent !! The Mohalla Sabha draft is to increase tensions and clash amongst citizens living in area. Given the structure, nothing will get done, and so each person will develop grudge against other It is classic divide and rule.

And can 2500 Mohalla expel CM? Hell no !! CM is unexepllable !!!

Digvijaya’s trusted babu to draft AAP's mohalla sabha law

NEW DELHI: Aam Aadmi Party chief Arvind Kejriwal on Wednesday told TOI that the former chief secretary of Madhya Pradesh, S C Behar, had been invited to the capital to work as an advisor to the government. Behar, who was to arrive in Delhi on Wednesday, would be actively involved in the party's key agenda of Swaraj by helping in the drafting of the legislation for mohalla sabhas.

Fifteen years after his retirement from administrative services, Behar is still considered by state bureaucrats a part of AICC general secretary Digvijaya Singh's inner coterie. It has, therefore, come as a shock to many how Behar could be accommodated as an adviser in the AAP government after Singh has been so vocal in his attacks on Kejriwal. ...

Tue Jan 07 2014 13:57:00 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Many factory owners voted for AAP, and asked ALL their labor to vote for AAP because of AAP promise to cut water/electricity rates by 50% !! They told labor that factories profits and labor's salary will increase if AAP comes into power !!!

I am asking AAP activists how they managed to fool so many people into believing that electricity/water rates will decrease. And this gets better and better !!!

Over 80% AAP activists were unpaid , committed, serious etc etc. And most are well learned. They had no intension of folloing even a mosquito. But they ended up fooling thousands of voters because they themselves were fooled. They fooled people, because AK and paid media had fooled them first !!! AK and paid media actually made them believe that water\electrcity rates will go down by 50%. And everyone will get free water. So when they were peddling this lie to others, they didnt laugh at all, and sounded dead serious !! And so voters agreed with them.

The slum dwellers were told that each family will get 200 coupons booklet every month, where each coupon will fetch them 100 liters from Delhi Jal Board tankers !!

And when activists told factory owners that electricity rates will decrease by 50%, the factory owners held meetings of AAP volunteers in factory compound to convince labor and their family members to vote for AAP !! The volunteers even said that "if electricity rates go half, factories profits will increase and so labor's salaries will also increase !!" And labor's electricity bill will go down.

IOW, it was NOT paid-media which directly fooled the labor. But paid-media convinced activists who convinced factory owner who together with activists convinced the labor voters !!

Thomas Jefferosn said in 1820 that "of people who read nothing and people who read only newspapers, those who read nothing know more and sound more sensible" !!! And TV-channels are more effective than newspapers. And apparently, the activists see more TV channels and also see TV-channels via youtube and so were brainwashed to bigger extent than labor. But then activists' personal presence fooled labor. The labor must have thought "well, if such educated and well earning people are working for free and losing salary for the volunteering work, AAP\AK must be made up of solid gold !!"

Now water prices and electricity prices for factories have gone up !!! So a small number of labor may get even laid off, if factory owner was on break even point.

The moral of story is that if you are an unpaid activist, you better be very careful. Delhi high court announces 47 new courts

Across all India, all CMs including NaMo want to tripple the number of courts or even make it ten times so that court delay can reduce. But judiciary doesnt increase number of courts. The main reason why judges dont increase number of courts has been influence of American elitemen. The foreign elitemen do not want number of courts in India to rise. But now that new Delhi CM is sponsored by American elitemen, the judges are acting fast to increase number of courts. If some other CM had asked for new courts, this would not have happened.

This step will increase the popularity of AK across Indian middle class.

see link a the end

Delhi high court announces 47 new courts

10-jan-2014

NEW DELHI: In a move that may help deal with the huge number of pending cases, the Delhi high court has announced the creation of 47 new courts in the capital.

Though the formal announcement of the expansion of courts came on Thursday, the decision had been finalized earlier this week. The new courts, which will come up in six district courts complexes from January 15, will deal with trial in heinous offences and civil matters including motor accidents cases.

The executive order, signed by high court registrar general, Sangita Dhingra Sehgal, also says due to the increase in the number of courts, 78 officers of Delhi Higher Judicial Services (additional district and sessions judges) and 150 officers of Delhi Judicial Services (civil judges and magistrates) have been transferred.

"The creation of new courts will certainly result in expediting the trials both in the district courts as well as in the HC," Sehgal said. Two senior trial court judges --Satinder Kumar Gautam and Brijesh Kumar Garg-- have been appointed ?as joint registrars of the high court to deal with civil matters and expedite cases. Along with the decision to increase the number of courts, HC has asked the district judges to also ensure ?that litigants are not inconvenienced and are informed within two weeks about which court their pending ?case has been re-assigned to so that ?"no inconvenience is caused to lawyers and litigants in the transfer of ?the cases". It has also instructed the judges who have either been promoted or transferred, to ensure they pronounce judgments in matters lying reserved for a verdict.

On Wednesday, Kejriwal and law minister Somnath Bharti met Chief Justice N V Ramana at his residence. Sources had said they exchanged views on expediting cases and reducing backlog.

timesofindia.indiatimes . com /city/delhi/Delhi-high-court-announces-47-new-courts/articleshow/28612788.cms

Read full post →

AK's free water, cheap electricity, useless anti-corruption hotline , going via metro, not putting red light etc etc nonsense steps. But THIS STEP IS REAL. Everyone who took sincere steps to reduce problems of India always found on day one that biggest problem is corruption/nepotism of judges and second biggest problem shortage of courts. Since 1998, I have been infoming voters/activists about Jury System to reduce corruption/nepotism problem, and that we should have 200,000 more courts than existing 18000. And many activists asked CM to raise number of courts. But they could not and did not. Nor did they give valid reasons why they cannot. And now that AK in 15 days has created 47 new courts, this step will fetch him HUGE popularity over NaMo and other leaders.

And now lets see some anatomy.

Whats the procedure to increase number of courts? The steps are -- (1) CM has to request High Court Chief judge (2) HCj then approves the request. Across all India, all CMs including NaMo want to tripple the number of courts or even make it ten times so that court delay can reduce. But apex-judiciary doesnt increase number of courts. The main reason why judges dont increase number of courts has been influence of American elitemen. The solution to that is Right to Recall judges and Jury System (which AK has opposed). The American elitemen do not want number of courts in India to rise. But now that new Delhi CM is sponsored by American elitemen, the judges are acting fast to increase number of courts. If some other CM had asked for new courts, this would not have happened. And this step will increase the popularity of AK across Indian middle class.

Now every citizen will ask a natural question --- why didnt NaMo increase number of courts in Gujarat? How come he can make bold demand that "all taxes be abolished" but he cannot ask for 80000 new courts across India and 6000 new courts in Gujarat? All these VALID questions will cause damage , unless real answers are given. And the real answer is --- no Indian leader can make a tall workable demand without approval of MNC-owners !!! And if an Indian leader makes a tall demand, then MNC-paid-media and MNC-influenced supreme-judges will roast him. Now sadly, BJP is not in position to give this REAL answer to voters. And so BJP leaders will have no answer to this question "why dont you triple the number of courts across states?" And so due to answerlessness, BJP leaders will lose points against AK on this "number of courts" issue.

Anyway, a good thing is good thing. My sincere congratulations and thanks to AK = AAP to create 47 new courts in Delhi. Its a good step, and will benefit people of Delhi and people across India. Since 1998 I have been asking that number of courts in India should go 10 times. On BRF, I have been writing about it since 2002. Sadly Nationalists never forced their leaders to take this step. So ABV didnt raise number of courts by any significant number and nor did any BJP CM. It is shame that Nationalists activists supported leaders who refused to increase number of courts.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sat Jan 11 2014 02:23:32 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

When TT comes, people will sell plots for Re 1 , and rest in gold !! And Govt will lose all stamp duty, capital gains tax and income tax, and get TT or only 2 paise !! Solution : pls focus on wealth tax.

Today, lets say A has a plot whose market value is Rs 1 crore and circle rate value is Rs 50 lakhs . Say A had bought plot for Rs 20 lakh in year 2000. Then indexation of Rs 20 lakhs will be Rs 30 lakhs. And so if A sells plot for Rs 1 crore, then A will pay 20% income tax = long term capital gains tax on (Rs 1 crore - Rs 30 lakhs) = Rs 70 lakhs = Rs 14 lakhs. And B will pay stamp duty of Rs 6 lakhs and since B has to show income of Rs 1 crore, on that income he would have had to pay some tax say at least Rs 20 lakhs or more. So total tax paid = Rs 14 lakhs + Rs 6 lakhs + Rs 20 lakhs = Rs 40 lakhs.

And if they sell for Rs 50 lakhs cheque and takes rest cash then tax collected will be

long term capital gains = 20% of Rs 20 lakhs = Rs 4 lakhs
stamp duty = 6% of 50 lakhs = Rs 3 lakhs
income tax on income of Rs 50 lakhs = about Rs 10 lakhs

Total = Rs 17 lakhs

So in existing tax regime, tax collected is Rs 17 lakhs to Rs 40 lakhs

And what if seller sells for Re 1 cheque and rest all cash? Then also income tax dept and stamp duty dept have powers to ASSUME that sale was Rs 40 lakhs, and collect taxes accordingly from buyer and seller. So no matter what, minimal tax will be collected as per today's laws.

Now if TT comes, the tax will go to not 2% = Rs 2 lakhs, but would become 2 paise. How?

In order to evade Rs 2 lakhs of tax, the buyer and seller will sell for Rs 1 cheque and rest by dollars. Now under new proposed TT regime, the TT department will have no power to make presumtions on minimal value of transaction, no power to go thru books and no powers to summon buyer-seller. So buyer and seller cant questions about gold they took or bought. The TT dept only looks at bank tarnsactions. And the bank tarsnaction is Re 1.

Also, persons can open gold accounts like we have gold certificate accounts. And give gold certificate as gift to each other. Now under TT regime, only bank transactions are taxed. The gift of gold certificates is via gold depository and NOT via bank. So no tax will be due.

Only way to stop this is to create TT-dept which iwll have power to make presumtions on value of all transfers and all transactions. Well, in that case, we are back to square one. All corruption in tax depts such as income tax, excise, sales tax etc is ONLY due to power to make presumption on value of sales, transfers, transactions etc If TT-dept is allowed to make presumptions

All in all, TT is TW = time-waste. And pls focus on wealth tax. Pls see my YT video on RightToRecallGroup channel , search for words tax, land OR wealth . Wealth tax of 1% on urand land above 250sqft per person will reduce land prices to 20% of what it is now, This will boost industry, trade, employment etc and so GDP will double in 3-4 years and without any raise in income tax rate, the income tax collected will double. With this, we can remove excise, service tax , sales tax, VAT etc. Only tax that will stay will be excise on vehicles to build roads and footpaths and customs.

Read full post →

Hi G Rajendra Kumar

I am creating this thread for you comments/Q etc.

Dear All, this thread is for comments of Jagadish Bhanderi . Others' comments will be deleted.

Pls glance at following suggestions of mine

====

1. Our comments will be useful for other volunteers. So pls never delete a comment , unless lines of that comment are moved into other comment.

2. Pls combine CONSECUTIVE (only consecutive comments as follows)
2a. Pls click on edit comment-1
2b. Pls copy the content of comment-2 into comment-1
2c. Then pls delete the lower comment-2

3. Pls do NOT combine non-consecutive comments.

4. To clarify what is consecutive comment and what is non-consecutive comment, let me give example. say

comment-1 is by you
comment-2 is by me
comment-3 is by you
comment-4 is by you

Then comment-3 and comment-4 are CONSECUTIVE comments and pls combine them

comment-1 and comment-3 are non-consecutive comments and so pls DO NOT combine them

5. Pls see my notes section on FB, and cntrl-F on SMS. There are notes on "citizens should order MPs via SMS". They are central to my campaign. Pls also download rahulmehta dot com slash 301.htm

6. Pls note that unlike other leaders, we NEVER ask activists to collect donations, hold our pictures, do naarebaazi etc. We only request activists to spread information about law-drafts, and we answers ALL questions for free. So I request ALL to putl comments and questions in PROPER textbook like Hindi or English, and NOT use SMS english, SMS abbreviation, and also not use informal writing style, as that makes reading time consuming and painful. Pls also avoid ALL CAPS posts.

7. If you have Q, pls first do ask Q to other volunteers. If you dont get satisfactory reply , then you are welcome to post it here. But before that, pls do ask Q to other volunteers.

To bring bad name to Direct aka Participatory Democracy, AK opposes structured Direct Democratic means such as complaining in via internet, SMS-voting on complaints and JurySys, and insists on chaos causing Jan Darbarr ; Ministers took complaints and did NOT give any receipts to the complainers !! Solution : Pls order MP via SMS to print Transparent Complain Filing draft given in section-1.3 of rahulmehta . com / 301.htm . Pls send #TransparentComplaintProcedure to your MP via SMS.

AK's goal is to give bad name to "Direct Democracy" and "Participatory Democracy"
. Which is why, he opposes ALL structured ways of Direct Democracy in the name of "give me some time, give me some time" and meanwhile AK expressly promotes chaotic ways like Jan Darbar, anti-corruption hot lines, SMS-voting with no reporting in SMS-counts and all this nonsense.

The Direct Democracy NOT implemented via chaotic forms like Mohalla Sabha or Town Meetings . It is implemented using STRUCTURED forms such as voting , referendum, right to recall and above Jury System. eg in ancient Greece, it was NOT that every dispute was resolved by public meeting of all 60000 Athens citizens. For a given complaint or disputes, 50, 100, 200, 300, 400, 500 , 1000 or 1500 citizens were called at random from whole voter list, and were asked to hear both sides and their witnesses for a few days, and then decide. There used to be standing body of 600 citizens chosen at random which would sit for whole month everyday to hear town's general issues. But while town or whole mohalla was NOT called . The town meeting used to be one a year for few days to make important announcements . The town meetings were not to resolve complaints and dispute.

One system which many AAP volunteers suggested to AK was from section-1.3 of rahulmehta . com / 301.htm . The system is to allow any complainer to personally come to city civic center or collector's office , and his clerk will scan his complain and put on website for fee. Later, it can be extended to video ii.e. person can state his complaint and recorded version will be put on CM's website. And any voter via SMS or personal visit to Talati office can add his name to complaint or remove his name. This way, there is well compiled list of which complaints are suffered by how many voters , and so forth. And Ministers or his staff can work on the complaints, and put replies on net, and inform via SMS to people see the replies.

But AK refused to implement any such TRANSPARENT website\SMS based system where complaint can come on website and voters can register their names via SMS. (proof : ask AK). After all dumb text on website doesnt fetch any publicity. And instead AK called all 1000s and lakhs of complainers across Delhi to attend Darbar. He did this only to drain out their energy. And create a show. And also to give a bad name to whole notion of participatory democracy !!

And if this goes on, people will soon say "I support corruption " !!

SOLUTION?

Solution I propose is that activists should order their MPs via SMS to create STRUCTURED means such as TCP etc. The draft for TCP is given in section-1.3 of rahulmehta . com /301.htm . Pls order your MP via SMS to print this draft, Pls send #TransparentComplaintProcedure to your MP via SMS.

For FAQs on "order your MPs via SMS", pls see

1. 303.016 : If 40 crores voters of India order MPs via SMS to print a law, then Mahatma Udham Singh will visit PM, and so the PM will print law ---- facebook . com / note.php ? note_id=10151190606916922

2. Mobile numbers of Loksabha MPs, so that we citizens can send them necessary orders via SMS. - 164 . 100 . 47 . 132 / LssNew/Members / membercontactdetail.aspx

3. 303:024 : Why sending necessary orders to PM\MP via SMS is better than slogan-shouting, neta-pujan and waiting for elections --- facebook . com / note.php ? note_id=10151192680806922

Solution : Pls order MP via SMS to print Transparent Complain Filing draft given in section-1.3 of rahulmehta . com / 301.htm . Pls send #TransparentComplaintProcedure to your MP via SMS.

AK's goal is to give bad name to "Direct Democracy" and "Participatory Democracy"
. Which is why, he opposes ALL structured ways of Direct Democracy in the name of "give me some time, give me some time" and meanwhile AK expressly promotes chaotic ways like Jan Darbar, anti-corruption hot lines, SMS-voting with no reporting in SMS-counts and all this nonsense.

The Direct Democracy NOT implemented via chaotic forms like Mohalla Sabha or Town Meetings . It is implemented using STRUCTURED forms such as voting , referendum, right to recall and above Jury System. eg in ancient Greece, it was NOT that every dispute was resolved by public meeting of all 60000 Athens citizens. For a given complaint or disputes, 50, 100, 200, 300, 400, 500 , 1000 or 1500 citizens were called at random from whole voter list, and were asked to hear both sides and their witnesses for a few days, and then decide. There used to be standing body of 600 citizens chosen at random which would sit for whole month everyday to hear town's general issues. But while town or whole mohalla was NOT called . The town meeting used to be one a year for few days to make important announcements . The town meetings were not to resolve complaints and dispute.

One system which many AAP volunteers suggested to AK was from section-1.3 of rahulmehta . com / 301.htm . The system is to allow any complainer to personally come to city civic center or collector's office , and his clerk will scan his complain and put on website for fee. Later, it can be extended to video ii.e. person can state his complaint and recorded version will be put on CM's website. And any voter via SMS or personal visit to Talati office can add his name to complaint or remove his name. This way, there is well compiled list of which complaints are suffered by how many voters , and so forth. And Ministers or his staff can work on the complaints, and put replies on net, and inform via SMS to people see the replies.

But AK refused to implement any such TRANSPARENT website\SMS based system where complaint can come on website and voters can register their names via SMS. (proof : ask AK). After all dumb text on website doesnt fetch any publicity. And instead AK called all 1000s and lakhs of complainers across Delhi to attend Darbar. He did this only to drain out their energy. And create a show. And also to give a bad name to whole notion of participatory democracy !!

And if this goes on, people will soon say "I support corruption " !!

SOLUTION?

Solution I propose is that activists should order their MPs via SMS to create STRUCTURED means such as TCP etc. The draft for TCP is given in section-1.3 of rahulmehta . com /301.htm . Pls order your MP via SMS to print this draft, Pls send #TransparentComplaintProcedure to your MP via SMS.

Sun Jan 12 2014 05:29:21 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Why Anna, AK, Congress leaders, BJP leaders, Indians political science professors oppose the Recallists' proposal to inform activists and citizens about Jury System of 12, 100, 1500 Jurors? Solution : activists should give newspaper-advt to inform citiziens about JurySys etc

Athens in 800 BC had system of deciding punishments in ALL cases by Juries of 25, 50, 100, 200, 300, 400, 500, 1000 or 1500 citizens between age of 30 years and 55 years chosen at random for citizenry of 60000 citizens. Bigger the crime, bigger the Jury. More powerful the criminal, bigger the Jury. And most Greek nations had similar systems. This system made Greece so powerful that Alexender with 50000 soldiers could conquer all the way from Greece to Turkey, Iraq, Iran, Afghanistan, Pakistan and upto western banks of Yamuna.

Today, in USA, most criminal and civil cases are judges by Jury of 12. But judges have a lot of powers, and so Jury System is not as robust as it was in Greece. But due to Right to Recall over police chief, Mayor, judges, public prosecutors etc the administration is fast as well less dominating.

We recallists have been informing activists and citizens about JurySys and RTR since 1998. But Anna, AK, all Congress leaders, all BJP leaders , all professors of political science, all professors etc have opposed this proposal that activists should be informed about this system. Instead, the insist that activists should be asked to spend time in naarebaazi, member making, cap wearing, mask wearing, jhadu weilding, janta darbar, vote grabbing etc etc. WHY?

The main reason is their dependence on elitemen's money for their paid-media coverage. These days, biggest expense of political movements, be us small time recallists or tall leaders like Anna, AK, Congress, BJP etc, is paid-media. We recallists also use paid-media ----- we give OFFICIALLY paid newspaper advt. AK, Anna depend on Jindal, American-elitemen and Missionaries to pay mediamen to cover them. Congress depends on desi elitemen and American elitemen for their paid-media-coverage. BJP depends on desi elitemen for their paid-media coverage. Now these elitemen do NOT want Indian activists to know about Jury System. And so AK has asked all its volunteers NOT to inform voters etc abdout JurySys (proof : ask AK). And AK also opposed the proposal to print Jury System clause in Lokpal draft (proof : see janlokpal draft).

Solution ? Solution I propose is that we recallists should give newspaper advt to inform voters about JurySys, RTR judges etc etc. And we should inform activists that AK is opposed to JurySys etc to the extent that he even opposes the proposal that activists should be informed about JurySys.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Jan 12 2014 06:38:43 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Spoils system in USA --- every time President changes, some 10000 officers lose jobs and new 10000 loyalist get appointed as new officers ; yer corruption before and after appointment is low ? Why? The power of Jury System .

USA has bottom and middle layers in administration where employees are recruited by written exams and have permanent tenure. But whole top layer is hire-fire. In India, when one party loses and another party win elections, only 800 MPs and 70 Ministers change. The IAS such as dept secretaries are only transferred. New ones dont come en-masse.

Whereas in USA, when President changes, each person at apex of each department except Military may lose job and new apex comes. About 10000 apex officials are removable. For a complete list of officials who are removed after each Presidential elections, pls see en.wikipedia . org / wiki/List_of_positions_filled_by_presidential_appointment_with_Senate_confirmation and en.wikipedia . org / wiki/United_States_Government_Policy_and_Supporting_Positions . The middle/bottom 10 lakhs officials stay. And similar structure is in state\district levels. It used to be called as spoils system.

Despite such wide appointment powers, the corruption during appointment and after appointment is low. Lobbying exists and so does favoritism, but it causes no disruption in lives of citizens and businesses. Despite massive changes in apex lawyer, the administration shows continuity and predictability.

The main reason is Jury System. The middle and bottom level officials work as poer laws and not as per whims of apex as they are answerable to Grand Juries and Juries. And Jury has continuity --- it comes from same population. The apex gets wide powers to re-organize , build something new, expand by giving incetives to middle and bottom. But apex cannot not disrupt or dominate, because middle/bottom will not allow disruption and domination because of fear of Jury.

All in all,lack of Jury System has cause immense damage in India. And let me proudly say that I and my colleagues have been trying to inform activists across India about Jury System using newspaper-advt, pamphlets etc . But I would also sadly say that we are the ONLY ones who are promoting JurySys in India. The information level remains low due to fire of elitemen who oppose JurySys. The elitemen manage to give huge publicity to publicity hungry people like Anna, AK etc , and these people , in order to gain publicity, agree to suppress information on JurySys. So activists dont get information about JurySys and so progress on JurySys is slow. Solution is that activists should dump time-wasters like AK, Anna etc and focus on promoting information on RTR, JurySys etc. The nationalists activists should also avoid timepass activities like morning walks at shakha, and focus on getting and spreading information on law-drafts.

=======

The so called newspapers and tv-channels are necessary to keep citizens informed. If nation has no newspaper and tv-channels, then it will become so weak compared to some other nation with tv-channels and newspapers that the nation with newspaper and tv-channels will devor the former. So newspapers , tv-channels etc are must.

Now these newspapers and TV-channels do not come out of thin air. It requires crores and crores of rupees and huge technical skills to print millions of copies and run 100s of channels.

The American-elitemen have compnanies whose employees have these skills. And Indian elitemen's companies' employees do NOT have these skills. The reason why American have more skills are JurySys, RTR etc which I have explained in chap-20 of rahulmehta . com / 301.htm . Amd the American-elitemen have also obtained huge money by looting Iraq, Libya etc.

The American elitemen have poured billions in Indian media. Today almost 100% English newspapers are under American-elitemen? And over 50% Indian language newspapers are also under American-elitemen? And some 80% of tv-news-channels are also under American-elitemen? And above all, only social media we have in India, are twitter\FB are both under CIA !!! And this didnt happen overnight. The increasing influence of American-elitemen became clear in 1991 when their puppets PVNR and MMS were made PM and Gandhi family was asked to walk away. And when Sushma Swaraj the then information minister demanded FDI in media in 2002, it was clear to all who were seeing and listening that BJP is apex is now compromised.

Now American elitemen are rich, but Indian Nationalist activists are not below poverty line. They have less money, but they too have some money. So how come we dont have even one nationalist newspaper? How come we dont have even one nationalist tv-channel with rating above 1%? So then how did we land in this soup?

IMO, the nationalist activists lost the game because they supported leaders over "good laws". And most of their leaders collected money, and they didnt invest much in paid-media, but only invested in gold and plots. One example is Pramod. Another is a Swadeshi ex-FinMin who and his family members aboe Rs 3000 crore of wealth in India and abroad !! And he will be NOT in top 20 richest nationalist leaders. If these leaders had spent even 5% of what they earned in Govt by right or wrong means, then we would be having one nationalist media.

SOLUTION?

Solution I propose is that activists should start giving newspaper advt to spread information on good law-drafts. And activists should ask voters to send orders to MPs via SMS to make necessary laws. When 40 crore voters send orders to MPs via SMS, either that law-draft will get passed, and imprisonment of mediamen will end. And if that law-draft doesnt pass, then citizens will know that they are in prison. This knowledge will help them in freeing them in times to come.

And a long term solution is to print RTR-Doordarshan-CEO-draft in Gazette.

Pls glance at following suggestions of mine

====

1. Our comments will be useful for other volunteers. So pls never delete a comment , unless lines of that comment are moved into other comment.

2. Pls combine CONSECUTIVE (only consecutive comments as follows)
2a. Pls click on edit comment-1
2b. Pls copy the content of comment-2 into comment-1
2c. Then pls delete the lower comment-2

3. Pls do NOT combine non-consecutive comments.

4. To clarify what is consecutive comment and what is non-consecutive comment, let me give example. say

comment-1 is by you
comment-2 is by me
comment-3 is by you
comment-4 is by you

Then comment-3 and comment-4 are CONSECUTIVE comments and pls combine them

comment-1 and comment-3 are non-consecutive comments and so pls DO NOT combine them

5. Pls see my notes section on FB, and cntrl-F on SMS. There are notes on "citizens should order MPs via SMS". They are central to my campaign. Pls also download rahulmehta dot com slash 301.htm

So I request ALL to putl comments and questions in PROPER textbook like Hindi or English, and NOT use SMS english, SMS abbreviation, and also not use informal writing style, as that makes reading time consuming and painful. Pls also avoid ALL CAPS posts.

7. If you have Q, pls first do ask Q to other volunteers. If you dont get satisfactory reply , then you are welcome to post it here. But before that, pls do ask Q to other volunteers.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Tue Jan 14 2014 06:48:45 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Almost all mechanical water meters in the world have this known problem that if air flows thru them with high pressure, then the meter will move. And when water flow ends, the pipe becomes vacant and gets filled with air. And so when water flow resumes, the air will be pushed out and so water meter will move. It creates an error some 2% in general. AK demonstrates this error to a crowd, and then tells the crowd that Delhi Jal Board has deliberately created the meters which will make them move even when air flows !!

Dear AAPians, your leader is a con. If this error is demonstrated in public, then also there is NO harm. But a truthful person will also say that such errors are across the world, and dont cause damage of more than 2%. And one should NOT make it look a pre-medidated theft on part of Jal Board.

The MNC-owners in their paid-media give no solutions to real problems Indians are facing. In fact, paid-media doesnt even give information to citizens about problems such as weakening of Indian Military, Bangladeshi, falling maths\science education etc. But paid-media does give lot of entertainment --- comedy, soft porn, sensation, thrillers, suspense etc. But then what do most nationalists offer? Mask wearing, slogan shouting, rallying, member making, morning walks etc. When someone asks for real solutions to real problems, all we get is more slogans and and more masks !!

IMO, the mess can end if and only if the nationalists activists study the REAL administrative solutions to real problems commons are facing, and inform the commons about these problems and solutions. Otherwise, I see paid-media winning

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Tue Jan 14 2014 10:27:46 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Hi Anish Sikarwar,

I am creating this thread for you comments/Q etc.

Dear All, this thread is for comments of Anish only . Others' comments will be deleted.

Pls glance at following suggestions of mine

====

1. Our comments will be useful for other volunteers. So pls never delete a comment , unless lines of that comment are moved into other comment.

2. Pls combine CONSECUTIVE (only consecutive comments as follows)
2a. Pls click on edit comment-1
2b. Pls copy the content of comment-2 into comment-1
2c. Then pls delete the lower comment-2

3. Pls do NOT combine non-consecutive comments.

4. To clarify what is consecutive comment and what is non-consecutive comment, let me give example. say

comment-1 is by you
comment-2 is by me
comment-3 is by you
comment-4 is by you

Then comment-3 and comment-4 are CONSECUTIVE comments and pls combine them

comment-1 and comment-3 are non-consecutive comments and so pls DO NOT combine them

5. Pls see my notes section on FB, and cntrl-F on SMS. There are notes on "citizens should order MPs via SMS". They are central to my campaign. Pls also download rahulmehta dot com slash 301.htm

6. Pls note that unlike other leaders, we NEVER ask activists to collect donations, hold our pictures, do naarebaazi etc. We only request activists to spread information about law-drafts, and we answers ALL questions for free. So I request ALL to putl comments and questions in PROPER textbook like Hindi or English, and NOT use SMS english, SMS abbreviation, and also not use informal writing style, as that makes reading time consuming and painful. Pls also avoid ALL CAPS posts.

7. If you have Q, pls first do ask Q to other volunteers. If you dont get satisfactory reply , then you are welcome to post it here. But before that, pls do ask Q to other volunteers.

Dear All,

If you wish NOT miss my posts, pls make "close friend" and also pls create a group called "serious political postors" and please put me and other postors in that group.

Many people see that they dont see my status. It is NOT conspiracy of facebook (I am too small for that), but it is because many AAPians etc mark my posts as spam . And so facebook doesnt show in others' newsfeed. Facebook has this logic of reducing visibility of posts marked as spam by too many postors.

So if you wish not to miss my posts, then solution is that you make me "close friend". OR pls create a friend group called "serious political postors" and please put me and other postors in that group. OR BOTH. In that case, each post of mine will be shown regardless of how many people mark it as spam.

Wed Jan 15 2014 03:06:00 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Dear All,

Pls focus on MAJOR systemic flaws of AAP = AK, and pls do NOT waste time on minor flaws. Otherwise, AK = AAP will fix minor flaws, and then tomato will be on your face.

A crafty leader will make series of small mistakes, and his sponsors will pay mediamen to highlight his small mistakes so that focus goes away and then he can fix the minor flaws and get his negative publicity overturned.

An example is lizard dropping its tail, so that predator keeps busy with tail and lizard can run away. Another example would be James Bond dropping ball bearings from his car when villain is chasing him, so that villain's car slips due to ball bearings.

What are major systemic flaws?

(1) AK is opposing Right to Recall Delhi Mayor law-draft, and also opposing RTR Delhi Corporator draft. He is also opposing RTR over Delhi District Education Officer.

(2) AK is oppose the proposal to print Right to recall Janlokpal clauses in the Delhi Janlokpal draft

(3) AK is opposing 1% wealth tax on land so that flat prices would come down.

(4) AK is also refusing to put land ownership records on the net.

(5) AK is opposing Right to Recall over Doordarshan Chairman so that paid-media's hold on Indian polity can go on.

(6) AK supports FDI in media and refuses that tv-channels and newspapers in Indian must be wholelly owned by Indians only.

(7) AK also opposes the proposal that slum dwellers be allowed to choose tanker provide by visiting Patwari office or SMS. And he opposes the proposal that ration card users be allowed to change ration card via SMS or personal visit to Patwari office.

(8) AK refused to keep SMS support in coming Mohalla Sabha draft

(9) And above all, AK opposes the proposal to bring Jury System in tribunals which come under Delhi Govt.

(10) And AK opposes narco-test in public even cases of murder, high tax evasion , high corruption etc. And AK oppooses death sentence on the 16-dec-2013 delhi bus case's "juvenile" criminal.

All in all, #AAPisCongress3 . AAP supports every wrong law which Congress/BJP does and AAP also opposes every good law which Congress\/BJP does. And AAP is worse, because Congress\BJP dont waste time of those who want system change. Whereas AK's main goal is to waste away time of activists who want system change.

IOW, pls take issues which briing out the FACT that AK\AAP are worse than Congress\BJP.

What are his minor flaws?

1. He didnt file case of Shiela Dixit --- Well, with flick of pen, AK will imprison Shiela Dixit or even Robert Vadra. In fact, I suspect that AK will soon do a drama of imprisoning Shiela Dixit and Robert Vadra. It is matter of just 5 months !! Loksabha election counting ends on 17-may-2014. And next day AK can release her. And give anyone 5% of money Shiela Dixit made, he may be willing to sit in prison for 5 years !! So if you focus on Shiela Dixit's cases and Robert Vadra's cases etc, then all you will get is imprisonment for 5 months, and thats all. And what you will lose is lakhs of votes across India !! And what would a case against Shiela Dixit fetch? Even here, AK opposes narco-test in public in corruption cases and AK opposes Jury Trial in corruption cases. So trial will be run by same corrupt/nepotic judges. So nothing will come out. Best case scenario is that lower court will convict in 3 months, so that AK can cut middle class votes. But after that, appeals in higher courts will go on forever.

2. Electricity rates wont go down till may-2014 --- He can call emergency Assembly session and reduce rates tomorrow. So the egg will be on face of those who say that "electricity rates wont fall till may-2014".

3. Janata Darbar --- he can call area wise meeting and that will be easy to manage

List is long.

So IMO, pls dont waste time on this minor issues.

As a rule of thumb, take an issue which will have LONG TERM POSITIVE impact on India. A narco-test on major corruption case in public will have long term benefit for India. Right to Recall will have long term positive impact on India. But mere cases against few leaders and few months' imprisonment will not have any long term impact. Also pls note, that whole game is only for 5 months !! For such small 5 months, anyone can do a drama. So pls dont raise any issue which small drama can solve.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Wed Jan 15 2014 15:54:10 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Why did Bangladeshi established Lokpal System in year 2004? Because of UN pressure.

Since 2011, I have been saying that this Lokpal with No Right to recall Lokpal andolan is only a cover to hide the fact that Lokpal system is coming due to UN = MNC\Missionary pressure. Essentially, if Lokpal law had come without fake Jan Andolan, then all MPs acroiss all parties would have lost respect of citizens. eg in 1991 when MPs signed WTO agreement, they had all lost respect of citizens.

Pls google "Bangladesh corruption independent commission wiki" ; the first pages I get is en.wikipedia . org / wiki/Anti_Corruption_Commission_Bangladesh . This page says "Anti Corruption Commission Bangladesh (Bengali: দুর্নীতি দমন কমিশন) often abbreviated: ACC (Bengali: দুদক) was formed through an act promulgated on 23 February 2004 that into force on 9 May 2004. Although initially, it could not make the desired impact, but immediately following its reconstitution in February 2007, the ACC began working with renewed vigor and impetus duly acceding to the United Nations' convention against corruption that was adopted by the General Assembly away back on 31 October 2003." !!!

So Bangladesh passed Lokpal like law, even though there was no Anna, AK, etc type anshan there. So what made Bangladeshi MPs pass this laws?

So BJP\Congress MPs got together and created this Janlokpal movement to cover the FACT that Lokpal law was being passed to meet UN = MNC-owners' demand. No wonder why Congress made Ramlila ground available in aug-2012 , and BJP-leaders asked RSS-worners en-masse to support Anna.

And as movement progressed, MNC-owners saw one more benefit --- why not convert Lokpal movement into political party and use it to further de-leverage nationalists, swadeshi , hinduvaadies in BJP.

===

Now its clear to me why AK opposed the proposal that IAC-activists should request voters to order MPs via SMS to print Janlokpal law. Because AK never wanted any real mass-movement. Congress\BJP wanted mass-movement to get justify the Lokpal law, but at the same time didnt want mass-movement to be successful, because otherwise that would have created 10s of mass-movements. So defeat in delhi election was used as pretext to speed up efforts of Lokpal law.

It was a nice reality show, where whole country participated. Everything was fine --- except that 3 years got wasted.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Thu Jan 16 2014 13:59:19 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Power of paid-media : 1000s of activists wasted their three years behind this "all hype , no design" AK = AAP

Small squabbles like AK vs Binny are non-issue. But a recent allegation from AAP-apex leader Yogendra Yadav that AAP MLA Vinod Binny has been bribed by BJP is important.

If AAP MLA Vinod Binny can be bribed by BJP, then what is going to stop Congress or BJP or foreigners from bribing 20 more AAP MLAs, coming Lokpals and coming AAP MPs? And if a person is bribed by foreigners, then his assets will be in Mauritius banks and wont be even visible in India. His behavior will become anti-India, and behavior would tell. But there wont be any visible link or sudden increase in assets that can create a proof, except narco-test in public. In such case , what remedy does AK propose to common voters and AAP-activists? NONE !!!

Since aug-2012, I have asked AAPians this question --- what will stop your 30 MLAs or your 300 MPs to sell out to foreigners the way Congress/BJP MPs have sold out? And AAPian have been saying "Please have faith in AK. The MLA-candidates and MP-candidates he will choose will be all bribe-free" !! Well, then how come Binny who was handpicked by AK sold out so soon?

IOW, it is pointless to change MLAs\MPs\PM\CMs etc without Right to Recall i.e. procedure by which voters can change PM\CM\MP\MLA anyday. This what Mahatma Chandrashekhar Azad had said in 1925 that "election without RTR will become a joke" !!

The Vinod Binny issue is IMO minor. Binny is nobody compared to sponsors of AK. The sponsors of AK can easily bring Binny under control. One way is to cut off media coverage to Binny. Binny has been jumping up and down because he gets media coverage. Once paid-media coverage ends, he will mellow down. Another way is to unleash IAS/IPS/judges on businesses Vinod Binny has. Once Binny faces a few inquiries against his business, he will mellow down. So AK's sponsors such as Jindal etc can bring Binny within his orbit in few days. But then , it is sponsors of AK who will call shots and not AK. Because without sponsors, AK cant bring Binny under control. AK doesnt have any legal power to control Binny. And AK he has any financial\media strength. And today it is just Binny --- what will stop 20 more MLAs from taking bribe, revolting against AK and removing him from CM-ship and installing someone else? And what will stop MPs from taking bribes from Congress, BJP or foreigners and rebelling in future? And what will stop Lokpals from taking bribes?

The parties such as Congress\BJP use following methods to control MP\MLA --- the MP\MLA often has businesses , or he is dependent on his relatives businesses. And his businesses or his relatives businesses can be damaged via govt inquiries which are conducted by officers under CM\PM. IOW, it is raw blackmail and nothing more !! The foreigners control their per MPs via businesses that MP has with foreigners. Or sometimes, the MP\MLA wants more senior position and so he himself controls himself.

AK has MLAs who have nothing and so they are out of control. But soon AK's sponsors will offer carrot and stick on Binny or AK or both and bring the quarrel under control.

But these means dont benefit us commons. For us, we need straight forward ways. eg we should DIRECTLY elect CM and have RTR over CM so that CM doesnt depend on MLAs. And we should have RTR over MLAs, so that if MLA mis-behaves, then voters can expel him. And so forth.

All in all, if a person gets term guarantee for 5 years for the position of Lokpal, or MLA\MP\CM\PM or 30 year term in IAS\IPS\judges, then all sort of misbehavior will happen.

But AAPians should see that their whole movement of setting aside RTR-DRAFTS and hoping that their Lokpals, MPs, MLAs, CMs, PM , judges, IAS, IPS etc will not sell out without RTR/Jury is sheer nonsense.

All in all, AK = AAP was 100% pure hype with NO design. There wasnt design to even address the simplest of all 60 year old problem --- what will stop majority of MPs from selling out?

The activists got swayed mainly because of paid-media . If AK had said this without drum beating by paid-media that "None of my MLA candidates will take bribes", then people would have laughed at him. But the paid-media first projected him as a superman. This created immense faith in adivists that AAP's MLAs, MPs and Lokpals will not sell out. And thus AK managed to kick aside RTR-issue.

All in all, this was 3 years wasted of 1000s of activists into creating just one more Congress.

Solution ? Solution I propose is that activists should meet as many AAPians as possible and ask them to focus on RTR-drafts FIRST. They should explain to AAPians that gving DRAFT doesnt need power. One can give RTR-drafts without any power in hand. And AK had promised to give RTR-drafts in apr-2011 in 2 months !! And now 2.75 years has passed. But still AK says same committee thing. RTR-draft should become FIRST priority.

In Greece in 800 BC, when a very powerful and wealthy person was involved in serious crime, a Jury of 500 to 1500 citizens from city of say population 60000 were selected at RANDOM and called to decide the case. The age was 35 years to 55 years. This was because such persons have pre-built nexuses with judges, IPS etc. And they can also bribe/coerce small number of Jurors. Now if Greece of 800 BC can have Jury Trial of 1500 Jurors, then surely India of today can have Jury Trial of 1500 Jurors.
