I need information on Estonia's law to reduce black money in land deals --- Govt can buy out a property sold, within 30 days, for 5% premium !! AK asks volunteers NOT to take interests in such laws !! Why? Solution? Please read on.

Say A sells land to B for say Rs 10 crore cheque white and Rs 5 crore cash. The cash will not come on sale deed. Now Estonia passed a law that if and land is sold, then within 30 days, Govt can pay 5% extra to the buyer and acquire the land !! IOW, the buyer will NOT keep black portion more than 5% !!! IOW, almost 100% of land sale will be by cheque only. This brings down the real estate price for hard working salaries men, as all their income is 100% white anyway.

Now can someone google more on this Estonian law?

====

And I want you all to see difference between Right to recall Group and AAP. I have been publicizing this forced buy-out law by paying 25% premium during sale (not if original owner doesnt wish to sell, but only after sale has occurred and within 30 days after sale) since oct-1998. Many AAP activists took this proposals to AK. AK asked activities to NOT to waste time on such proposals and focus on cap-wearing , slogan shouting etc (ask AK). AK repeatedly kept sayng that such tasks should be left to "committee of experts" only and activists and citizens must NOT take any interest in such proposals !!! And finally, AK refuses to add this "forced buy out" proposal to reduce black money in real estate in AAP delhi manifesto !!

AK has also opposed 1% wealth tax on urban land above 250sqft per person. This is mainly because some of the biggest land owners like Jindals, Sonia and MNCs are paying mediamen for his campaign. This 1% wealth tax will reduce land hoarding in cities and bring down land prices to 10% of what is now and bring down construction prices by about 30%. This will reduce umemployment and reduce inflation as well.

All in all, AAP = AK does NOT ask activists to study law-drafts which would improve India. Their main focus is on cutting away middle class voters only. The activists who wish serious law-drafts in India should NOT waste their time after AAP and focus on asking voters to send orders to MPs via SMS to print those law-drafts.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Mon Jan 20 2014 11:38:24 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

articles.timesofindia.indiatimes . com/2014-01-18/india/46324015_1_article-370-rajnath-singh-narendra-modi

[b]No objection to Article 370 if it helps J&K develop: Rajnath Singh[/b]

NEW DELHI: Reflecting a change in stance, BJP president Rajnath Singh on Saturday said his party has no objection to Article 370 which grants special status to Jammu & Kashmir if it helps in the development of the state. "J&K is a very serious, very sensitive region for us ... If J&K develops because of Article 370, then we have no objection in keeping it," he said in his inaugural addresses at the two-day National Executive meet of the party.

This marks a change in BJP's stand as it has been consistently demanding repeal of the Article and has aggressively campaigned for it. However, in a bid to not appear as giving up the issue, Rajnath Singh went on to add that if the Article 370 was a "conspiracy" to stop the state from joining Indian mainstream, then his party would not accept it under any circumstances.

The party's prime ministerial candidate Narendra Modi had earlier last month stated at a rally in Jammu that there should be at least a debate on whether or not Article 370 has benefitted the state. "As per Constitution, the debate will continue whether the Article 370 should end or continue - at least there should be debate whether Article 370 has benefitted J&K or not," the Gujarat chief minister had said at the rally on December one.

BJP had then rejected suggestions that Modi had softened the party's stand on the issue. "It would be incorrect for anyone to interpret BJP's challenge for a debate on this issue as a softening of stand on Article 370," Leader of Opposition in Rajya Sabha Arun Jaitley had said. BJP has long kept its demand of Article 370's abrogation on the back burner in its quest for allies, who have been wary of its hardline stand, and the elevation of Modi had fuelled concerns that BJP would revert to its original demand under his command.

=================== end of newspaper news ================

So what is next? BJP will support Go-Hatya if it increases growth? BJP will make Kashmir independent as USA has been demanding, if that raises US investments in India and raises growth? BJP will support of seccession of Manipur, Nagaland etc if Missionarues invest $200 billion in rest of India to promote growth? BJP will supprt missionaries cross India if they invest $200 billion more to promote growth? And BJP will let Asam become part of Bangladeshi if Saudi Arabia agrees to give 10% discount on oil, because cheaper oil will promote growth? And how about rebuilding babri mosque for growth?

So folks, wake and smell coffee. It is NOT about muslims votes , but Saud money. Muslims will never voted for BJP. Its all about Saud money i.e. Saud money coming to media. Let me explain.

The Saudies pay loads of money to paid-Rajdeep Sardesi etc. And they have promoted AAP to a level that AAP can take away crores of middle class voters away from BJP. The recent delhi anshan fiasco may change the scene, but scene till recently was tense for BJP. And so BJP is offered a deal by Saudies ---- cool down your stand on art-370, RJB, go-hatya, UCC etc etc. All in all, AAP threat is working on BJP. One by one, BJP is making all issues orphans.

Who who wish to repeal art-370 should start looking beyod BJP.

BJP drops income tax abolition idea

As BJP prime ministerial candidate Narendra Modi called for reforms to check "tax terrorism" in the country, top leaders of the party were clear they would not root for abolition of income tax as demanded by Baba Ramdev. Instead, the BJP would pledge to undertake revamp or rationalization of taxes if it was voted to power at the Centre.

2. Right to Recall District Public Prosecutors

3. Jury Trial to decide cases on punishment for sale and consumption of opium, liquor, commercial sex etc

4. Right to recall Home Minister, CM

These law-drafts will result into defacto zoning and legalization of all three !! Because across world, across time, when Jury or Panchayats were in-charge of punishing people for these crimes, the Juries and Panchayats never punished anyone for these "crimes" in their zones and punished criminals only outside the zones. This reduced nuisance to residents to zero. In case you guys have seen Chanakya serial, it shows same thing. There were dens where opium, bhaang, liquor, commercial sex etc was there. But rest of the town didnt have that nuisance.

And if two persons involve in debate and post 10s of comments, then it gives too many notifications to everyone. And finally, people click "stop notifications" and then they will also stop getting notifications on my comments in their thread. So I lose audience.

Besides, to speak to a specific pastor, you can always start thread on your wall or his wall. So my "no cross talk" rule doesnt curb your freedom of speech.

In 1820, Mahatma Thomas Jefferson , and he was a True Mahatma, told me following about paid-media

(remove spaces or see link in first comment )

fourwinds10 . net /siterun_data/media/mainstream_media/news.php ? q=1334715256

1. The man who never looks into a newspaper is better informed than he who reads them, inasmuch as he who knows nothing is nearer to truth than he whose mind is filled with falsehoods and errors. He who reads nothing will still learn the great facts, and the details are all false.

2. I deplore... the putrid state into which our newspapers have passed and the malignity, the vulgarity, and mendacious spirit of those who write for them... These ordure’s are rapidly depraving the public taste and lessening its relish for sound food. As vehicles of information and a curb on our functionaries, they have rendered themselves useless by forfeiting all title to belief... This has, in a great degree, been produced by the violence and malignity of party spirit.

3. As for what is not true, you will always find abundance in the newspapers.

4. Advertisements... contain the only truths to be relied on in a newspaper.

5. Nothing can now be believed which is seen in a newspaper. Truth itself becomes suspicious by being put into that polluted vehicle.

Problems of India

There is no Taco Bell in Ahmedabad. In fact, there is only one Taco vell in whole of India --- and its in Banglaore.

And even McDonanld in India lack good menu. They dont serve Apple Pie. There is a serious problem in service too. A few days back, I went to McDonald at Panchvati CG Road, Ahmedabad to have Coffee and Pancake. And they told me that their Coffee machine was broke for over 3 days !! So I went to McDonald at Vijay Char Rasta, Ahmedabad and I found that they didnt have mix to make Pancake. And so I went to third McDonand at City, Gold, Ashram Road, Ahmedabad and they also didnt have Pancake mix !! I wish McDonand can fix their outlets.

Moral of the story is :

Sometimes I run into discussion on "problems in India" with people and they dont even know or wish to know about problems like weakening of Indian Military, Bangladeshi, poverty, rising conversions, rising naxalism, etc. And they instead insist on focusing on problems like lack of parking space, lack of good gardens etc etc. So I made a point to raise above issues of lack of Taco Bell and service problems in McDonalds before them

Fri Jan 31 2014 10:26:45 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

NaMo accused of giving huge financial favors to Venkiah Naidu's company

This website is anti-Modi and gives many lies. So I cant be sure it is true. But allegation is that NaMo gave use rights over 600 million sqm = 6000 million sqft = 600 crore sqft = 600 sqkm = an area as big as Mumbai for 100 years for meter Rs 15 to a company !!! And Mr. Venkiah Naidu's relatives own the company !!! Now this allegation may be all wrong as this webdite has printed many lies against NaMo in past. Solution I propose is MRCM and Right to Recall CM. If land rent were to directly go to citizens, then citizens should not allow a penny of such loss due to favoritism.

truthofgujarat . com / narendra-modi-pocketed-former-advani-aide-venkaiah-naidu-win-cronies-politics-modi-style/

How Narendra Modi pocketed former Advani aide Venkaiah Naidu

Not many would have taken notice of a large piece of land being transferred to a company called the Archian Chemicals in remote Kutchh area on a 99 year lease for salt based chemical project in 2005. The only noticeable factor was the size and the rate of lease. The area of 600 miliion square meter of land was rented away by the Government of Gujarat at the rate of just 15 paise per annum! Perhaps this deal would have gone unnoticed, had the company not failed to start their declared project within the two years stipulated time. Since the company did not start the work till 2014, it attracted the penalty of cancellation of the lease and reverting the land back to the Government.

However, what is bizarre is that, Government instead of the starting the procedure to recover the land from the defaulting company, suddenly, accepted a fresh application from the same company which was processed by the Gujarat Maritime Board. It has now been realized that the Archian Company belonged to the family of Venkaiah Naidu and that was the reason for giving away 600 million square meter of virtually free Government land. Gujarat’s leading vernacular daily, Gujarat Samachar, reported on 30th January, 2014, that the Gujarat Maritime Board has given a “in-principle” sanction to the Company to build a 150 long Jetty at the Kori-creek in Kutchh.

The application for sanction of the Jetty is now pending in the office of the Chief Minister Mr. Modi. What has surprised most of the people is the way Government has overlooked the penalty clause and instead of recovering the land given originally, the Company is once again given a reward of a Jetty. The most shocking aspect of the new deal is the sensitivity of the Kori-creek area which is at Indo-Pak border and a dispute exists between the two countries regarding the creek!

The reason for this generosity of the Chief Minister towards Venkaiah is not very difficult to find. Since 2009, Modi had been facing the tough resistance from his mentor LK Advani and his close confidants in his march to reach the top . M Venkaiah Naidu, Sushma Swaraj, Ananth Kumar, the close confidants of L K Advani had refused to accept Modi as their super boss till the Parliamentary meeting on 13th September, 2013. Shri LK Advani as a protest refused to attend the meeting. Yet the meeting on 13th September,2013 that was attended by 10 of the 12 members of the Parliamentary Board namely, Rajnath Singh, Murli Manohar Joshi, M Venkaiah Naidu, Nitin Gadkari, Sushma Swaraj, Arun Jaitley, Narendra Modi, Ananth Kumar, Thawarchand Gehlot and Ramlal anointed Modi as the Prime Ministerial candidate of BJP. The resistance of the dissidents melted away as Modi waved his magic-wand.

Mr Venkaiah Naidu was perhaps the closest to Advani. As the former President of BJP, he did have a clout in BJP. On 30th August, 2009, Venkaiah Naidu, was quoted saying " Advaniji is the leader. He will continue to lead the party; there is no proposal to change anybody, we are surprised by these reports about succession plan, stepping down, appointment of new president, new leader, RSS dictation– all these things… are totally baseless. They are speculative and imaginative."

But Kori-creek tells the whole story. Quite apart from the inner party corruption, the nation deserves to know why a place as sensitive as Kori-creek is being handed over to a private company of Venkaiah Naidu? Can the security of the nation be bartered for the exchange of a vote for the Prime Ministerial candidate?

Read full post →
